TASK MIND-BV — Gemma vision vs YOLO: room, person, distance (research only)

QUESTION: On the robot's photos, how well does Gemma 4 E2B vision name the room, see a person and judge the
person's distance, compared with the robot's YOLO detections plus simple rules?

DO NOT: change any deployed file or any existing benchmark folder (import; read frames and
results.json only); start RobotCam, YOLO, motors or the mission; use MTP; change any prompt, rule, label
or threshold after the first Gemma call; commit or push. Accuracy is deterministic (temperature 0):
agent resident allowed; timings NOT MEASUREMENTS.

PHOTOS: benchmark/relate_anything/quality1/runs/20261017T213604Z/frames/, sorted by name, numbered 1–70.
Use ids 1–60 and 66 (61 photos; 61–65 and 67–70 are duplicates). Check each frame's SHA-256 against
results.json before any call.

KEY (owner-confirmed 2026-11-03). Write to /termux-home/mind_bv_key/<UTC>/bv_key.json OUTSIDE the repo,
SHA-256 before any Gemma call; it enters the repo only after scoring, on my decision.
Fields: room, person (yes/no), distance (close <1 m, medium 1–2 m, far >2 m, none).
 1-4 living room|no|none     5 bathroom|no|none     6 hallway|no|none     7 kitchen|no|none
 8 living room|no|none       9 bedroom|no|none
 10,11,12 living room|yes|close      13,14,15 living room|yes|medium      16 living room|yes|far

 24,25,26 bedroom|no|none

METHODS (all fixed now):
 Y  YOLO + rules, from results.json detections of run 20261017T213604Z (no new YOLO run; report which
    detector model, input size and confidence threshold produced them, from quality1.py/run.json).
    Person = any "person" detection. Distance = the robot's bucket of the person with the largest box:
    "very close"/"close" -> close, "medium" -> medium, "far" -> far; none if no person.
    Room, first match: toilet -> bathroom; bed -> bedroom; refrigerator, oven or microwave -> kitchen;
    couch, tv or dining table -> living room; else unknown. (YOLO has no hallway cue.)
 V70  Gemma vision, image tokens 70.   V140  Gemma vision, image tokens 140.
 VY70 Gemma vision, image tokens 70, plus a line listing method Y's detections
      ("Objects the robot's detector found: <class> (<position>, <distance>), ..." or "none").
 Server: exactly BC's b11716 command (mem1 setup_q4 flags, b11716 binary, --mmproj Q8 projector,
 pins as BC), with --image-min-tokens/--image-max-tokens = 70 or 140 per launch; no --slot-save-path.
 Requests: /v1/chat/completions, GGUF template, chat_template_kwargs enable_thinking false (as BC K4),
 temperature 0, top_k 1, seed 0, max_tokens 32. Image as in VIS-3D (frame bytes unchanged).
 Prompt (verbatim; VY70 inserts its detector line before "Answer"):
   Look at this photo from a small home robot's camera.
   Answer in exactly three lines:
   Room: living room, kitchen, bedroom, bathroom, hallway or unknown
   Person: yes or no
   Distance: close (under 1 m), medium (1 to 2 m), far (over 2 m) or none
   Give only these three lines.
 Parsing: case-insensitive, one value per line from the allowed lists; anything else = UNPARSED for
 that field (counts as wrong; reported separately). Keep every reply verbatim.

PROCEDURE: launch A (70 tokens): V70 then VY70; launch B (140 tokens): V140. Blocks of at most 31
 photos, fixed order, 60 s pause between blocks. Record server stop exit codes (b11716 history, #145).

METRICS per method: room accuracy (61), person accuracy (61), distance accuracy on the 44 photos with a
person (a "no" or wrong bucket = wrong), all-three-correct count, confusion matrices, UNPARSED counts,
list of wrong ids per field.
PASS (fixed now): the best vision method by room accuracy has room >= 0.80 AND its person accuracy >= Y's.
Report PASS/FAIL, plus every method's numbers.

FILES: benchmark/gemma_vision/bv_compare/{bv.py, test_bv.py, README.md}; offline tests with a fake server
(Y rules incl. priority and largest-box choice, parsing incl. UNPARSED, metrics, PASS rule, custody).
Run folder ~/robot_runs/mind_bv/<UTC>/ with run.json, requests.jsonl, report.txt, artifacts.sha256.
Known B0 limitation (interrupt/cleanup finalization) is out of scope; the Reviewer may note it as P3.

STEPS: implement -> offline tests -> one phone run -> invoke the Reviewer automatically per WORKFLOW in a
new timestamped review folder (source-only method if the Codex sandbox cannot execute in proot; say so).
If Codex is at capacity, wait 10 min and retry once; then report INCOMPLETE -> freeze -> return the

OWNER DECISIONS (2026-11-03, before any Gemma call, answers to the Coder's questions):

   -> "Key is right: 40". Distance accuracy is over the key's 40 person photos.
2. "results.json keeps no box, position or distance for photos with only one detection (only 'classes').
   That affects person photos 10, 12, 43 and 44, so method Y has no distance for them, and VY70's detector
   line can't show position or distance there. A new YOLO run is forbidden."

   class list.

## D. Full numbered sources (new files)
BEGIN benchmark/gemma_vision/bv_compare/bv.py (393 lines)
   1  #!/data/data/com.termux/files/usr/bin/python
   2  """MIND-BV: on the robot's photos, how well does Gemma 4 E2B vision name the room, see a person and judge the person's
   3  distance, compared with the robot's YOLO detections plus simple rules? Research only: no motors, camera, YOLO run,
   4  mission or MTP. Accuracy is deterministic (temperature 0; an agent may be resident); timings NOT MEASUREMENTS.
   5  
   6  Photos: the M2-Q1 frames (relate_anything/quality1/runs/20261017T213604Z/frames/), sorted by name and numbered 1-70;
   7  ids 1-60 and 66 are used (61-65 and 67-70 are duplicates). Each frame's SHA-256 is checked against that run's
   8  results.json before any call. Key: the owner's bv_key.json, outside the repo (refused inside it, checked before
   9  reading), SHA-256 pinned in KEY_SHA256. Fields room, person (yes/no), distance (close/medium/far/none).
  10  
  11  Methods (fixed before the first Gemma call):
  12    Y     YOLO + rules from results.json's detections (no new YOLO run). Person = any "person" detection. Distance =
  13          the robot's bucket of the person with the largest box (very close/close -> close, medium, far); none
  14          without a person. Photos with one detection keep only 'classes' in results.json (no box, no bucket): a
  15          person there gets distance UNAVAILABLE, scored wrong (owner decision 2026-11-03). Room, first match in
  16          ROOM_RULES, else unknown (no hallway cue).
  17    V70 / V140   Gemma vision with 70 / 140 image tokens, PROMPT.
  18    VY70  V70 plus a line listing Y's detections (detector_line) inserted before "Answer".
  19  Server: BC's b11716 command (mem1.command(2048), setup_q4 flags, b11716 llama-server, BC's pins) plus --mmproj Q8_0
  20  and --image-min-tokens = --image-max-tokens = 70 or 140; no --slot-save-path. Requests: /v1/chat/completions (GGUF
  21  template), VIS-3D's image payload (frame bytes unchanged) with PROMPT, max_tokens 32, temperature 0, top_k 1, seed 0,
  22  chat_template_kwargs enable_thinking false (as BC K4).
  23  Launch A (70 tokens): V70 then VY70; launch B (140): V140. Blocks of at most 31 photos in id order, a 60 s pause
  24  between blocks of one launch. The server's exit code after SIGTERM is reported; nonzero is a FINDING (#145).
  25  
  26  Parsing (parse): per line, '*' removed, split at the first ':'; label and value lowercased and stripped, one
  27  trailing '.' dropped. A field needs exactly one line with its label and a value in ALLOWED (distance also accepts
  28  the option text as offered, e.g. "close (under 1 m)"); anything else is UNPARSED (counted wrong, reported).
  29  Metrics per method: room and person accuracy (61), distance accuracy on the key's person photos (a "no" or a wrong
  30  bucket is wrong), all three correct, confusion matrices (distance over all 61), UNPARSED counts, wrong ids.
  31  PASS: a vision method with the highest room accuracy has room >= 0.80 and person accuracy >= Y's.
  32  
  33  Usage: bv.py --key PATH   (Termux python; from proot Debian or native Termux)
  34  Writes ~/robot_runs/mind_bv/<UTC>/: run.json, requests.jsonl, server_A.log, server_B.log, report.txt,
  35  artifacts.sha256. Known limitation carried over from B0: an interrupt or a server-stop error can skip report.txt
  36  and artifacts.sha256 (completed rows stay in requests.jsonl).
  37  """
  38  import argparse
  39  import base64
  40  import json
  41  import os
  42  import signal
  43  import subprocess
  44  import sys
  45  import time
  46  from collections import Counter
  47  from pathlib import Path
  48  
  49  sys.dont_write_bytecode = True
  50  HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
  51  REPO = HERE.parents[2]
  52  sys.path.insert(0, str(REPO / 'benchmark/mind_picker/bc_build'))
  53  import bc  # noqa: E402  BC's command, pins, VIS-3D payload; imports b0, mem1, vis3d
  54  
  55  b0, mem1, cr, vis3d, sha = bc.b0, bc.mem1, bc.cr, bc.vis3d, bc.sha
  56  detect_person = sys.modules['detect_person']  # imported by coresidency (cr); read for CONF/IOU/MODELS only
  57  inside_repo = bc.b2.b1.inside_repo
  58  Q1 = REPO / 'benchmark/relate_anything/quality1/runs/20261017T213604Z'
  59  FRAMES_DIR, RESULTS, MANIFEST = Q1 / 'frames', Q1 / 'results.json', Q1 / 'manifest.json'
  60  IDS = list(range(1, 61)) + [66]
  61  KEY_SHA256 = '456f633bd9145edc9a4259171ad23f5957cc66ce11e3bd30983fcd92ad470edb'  # owner key, 2026-11-03
  62  OUT_ROOT = Path(cr.HOME) / 'robot_runs' / 'mind_bv'
  63  CTX = 2048
  64  LAUNCHES = [('A', 70, ['V70', 'VY70']), ('B', 140, ['V140'])]
  65  BLOCK, PAUSE_S = 31, 60
  66  FIELDS = ('room', 'person', 'distance')
  67  ALLOWED = {'room': ('living room', 'kitchen', 'bedroom', 'bathroom', 'hallway', 'unknown'),
  68             'person': ('yes', 'no'), 'distance': ('close', 'medium', 'far', 'none')}
  69  OFFERED = {'close (under 1 m)': 'close', 'medium (1 to 2 m)': 'medium', 'far (over 2 m)': 'far'}
  70  ROOM_RULES = [({'toilet'}, 'bathroom'), ({'bed'}, 'bedroom'), ({'refrigerator', 'oven', 'microwave'}, 'kitchen'),
  71                ({'couch', 'tv', 'dining table'}, 'living room')]
  72  BUCKET = {'very close': 'close', 'close': 'close', 'medium': 'medium', 'far': 'far'}
  73  UNPARSED, UNAVAILABLE = 'UNPARSED', 'UNAVAILABLE'
  74  PROMPT_HEAD = "Look at this photo from a small home robot's camera."
  75  PROMPT_TAIL = ('Answer in exactly three lines:\n'
  76                 'Room: living room, kitchen, bedroom, bathroom, hallway or unknown\n'
  77                 'Person: yes or no\n'
  78                 'Distance: close (under 1 m), medium (1 to 2 m), far (over 2 m) or none\n'
  79                 'Give only these three lines.')
  80  VISION_METHODS = ('V70', 'V140', 'VY70')
  81  METHODS = ('Y',) + VISION_METHODS
  82  
  83  
  84  # ---------------------------------------------------------------- inputs
  85  
  86  def load_key(path, want=KEY_SHA256):
  87      """{id: {frame, room, person, distance}}; refused inside the repo (checked before reading) or on a hash change."""
  88      if inside_repo(path):
  89          raise SystemExit(f'refused: {path} is inside the repository (key custody)')
  90      data = Path(path).read_bytes()
  91      if sha(data) != want:
  92          raise SystemExit(f'refused: {path} SHA-256 {sha(data)[:12]} != pinned {want[:12]}')
  93      key = {int(k): v for k, v in json.loads(data).items()}
  94      if sorted(key) != IDS:
  95          raise SystemExit(f'refused: key ids are not {IDS}')
  96      return key
  97  
  98  
  99  def photos(results, frames):
 100      """{id: results.json record} for IDS; frames = sorted frame names (1-based numbering)."""
 101      by_file = {Path(p['frame_file']).name: p for p in results['photos']}
 102      return {i: by_file[frames[i - 1]] for i in IDS}
 103  
 104  
 105  def frame_problems(ph, key, frames_dir=FRAMES_DIR):
 106      bad = []
 107      for i, p in ph.items():
 108          f = frames_dir / Path(p['frame_file']).name
 109          if key[i]['frame'] != f.name:
 110              bad.append(f'id {i}: key frame {key[i]["frame"]} != {f.name}')
 111          elif not f.is_file() or sha(f.read_bytes()) != p['frame_sha256']:
 112              bad.append(f'id {i}: {f.name} SHA-256 != results.json {p["frame_sha256"][:12]}')
 113      return bad
 114  
 115  
 116  # ---------------------------------------------------------------- method Y
 117  
 118  def classes(p):
 119      return [d['class_name'] for d in p['detections']] if p.get('detections') else list(p.get('classes') or [])
 120  
 121  
 122  def area(d):
 123      x1, y1, x2, y2 = d['box_xyxy']
 124      return (x2 - x1) * (y2 - y1)
 125  
 126  
 127  def y_pred(p):
 128      cls = classes(p)
 129      room = next((r for names, r in ROOM_RULES if names & set(cls)), 'unknown')
 130      if 'person' not in cls:
 131          return {'room': room, 'person': 'no', 'distance': 'none'}
 132      boxes = [d for d in p.get('detections') or [] if d['class_name'] == 'person']
 133      dist = BUCKET[max(boxes, key=area)['distance']] if boxes else UNAVAILABLE
 134      return {'room': room, 'person': 'yes', 'distance': dist}
 135  
 136  
 137  def detector_line(p):
 138      if p.get('detections'):
 139          items = [f'{d["class_name"]} ({d["position"]}, {d["distance"]})' for d in p['detections']]
 140      else:  # one-box photos: class only (owner decision 2026-11-03)
 141          items = [f'{c} (position unknown, distance unknown)' for c in classes(p)]
 142      return "Objects the robot's detector found: " + (', '.join(items) or 'none')
 143  
 144  
 145  # ---------------------------------------------------------------- vision requests
 146  
 147  def prompt(method, p):
 148      return '\n'.join([PROMPT_HEAD] + ([detector_line(p)] if method == 'VY70' else []) + [PROMPT_TAIL])
 149  
 150  
 151  def body(method, p, jpeg_b64):
 152      b = vis3d.image_payload(None, jpeg_b64)  # VIS-3D: image then text, greedy, nothing cached
 153      b['messages'][0]['content'][1]['text'] = prompt(method, p)
 154      return {**b, 'max_tokens': 32, 'chat_template_kwargs': {'enable_thinking': False}}
 155  
 156  
 157  def command(tokens):
 158      cmd = mem1.command(CTX)
 159      cmd[cmd.index(mem1.SERVER)] = bc.SERVER
 160      return cmd + ['--mmproj', vis3d.MMPROJ, '--image-min-tokens', str(tokens), '--image-max-tokens', str(tokens)]
 161  
 162  
 163  def run_method(post, launch, method, ph, pid, emit, frames_dir=FRAMES_DIR, first_block=True):
 164      """Blocks of BLOCK photos in id order, PAUSE_S between blocks (not before the launch's first). Returns rows."""
 165      rows = []
 166      for n, k in enumerate(range(0, len(IDS), BLOCK)):
 167          if n or not first_block:
 168              time.sleep(PAUSE_S)
 169          for i in IDS[k:k + BLOCK]:
 170              p = ph[i]
 171              b = body(method, p, base64.b64encode((frames_dir / Path(p['frame_file']).name).read_bytes()).decode())
 172              row = {'launch': launch, 'method': method, 'block': n, 'id': i, 'frame': Path(p['frame_file']).name,
 173                     'pid': pid, 'pid_start': b0.pid_start(pid), 'prompt': b['messages'][0]['content'][1]['text']}
 174              t0 = time.perf_counter()
 175              try:
 176                  res = post('/v1/chat/completions', b)
 177                  msg = res['choices'][0]['message']
 178                  row.update(content=msg.get('content'), reasoning_content=msg.get('reasoning_content'),
 179                             finish_reason=res['choices'][0].get('finish_reason'), usage=res.get('usage'))
 180              except Exception as e:  # recorded; the run is INVALID
 181                  row['error'] = f'{type(e).__name__}: {e}'
 182              row['wall_s'] = round(time.perf_counter() - t0, 3)
 183              emit(row)
 184              rows.append(row)
 185      return rows
 186  
 187  
 188  # ---------------------------------------------------------------- scoring
 189  
 190  def parse(reply):
 191      found = {f: [] for f in FIELDS}
 192      for line in (reply or '').splitlines():
 193          label, sep, value = line.replace('*', '').partition(':')
 194          label, value = label.strip().lower(), value.strip().lower()
 195          if sep and label in found:
 196              found[label].append(value[:-1].strip() if value.endswith('.') else value)
 197      out = {}
 198      for f, vals in found.items():
 199          v = OFFERED.get(vals[0], vals[0]) if len(vals) == 1 else None
 200          out[f] = v if v in ALLOWED[f] else UNPARSED
 201      return out
 202  
 203  
 204  def metrics(pred, key):
 205      pids = [i for i in IDS if key[i]['person'] == 'yes']
 206      ok = {f: [i for i in IDS if pred[i][f] == key[i][f]] for f in ('room', 'person')}
 207      ok['distance'] = [i for i in pids if pred[i]['person'] == 'yes' and pred[i]['distance'] == key[i]['distance']]
 208      n = {'room': len(IDS), 'person': len(IDS), 'distance': len(pids)}
 209      return {'acc': {f: len(ok[f]) / n[f] for f in FIELDS}, 'correct': {f: len(ok[f]) for f in FIELDS}, 'n': n,
 210              'all3': sum(all(pred[i][f] == key[i][f] for f in FIELDS) for i in IDS),
 211              'wrong': {f: [i for i in (pids if f == 'distance' else IDS) if i not in ok[f]] for f in FIELDS},
 212              'unparsed': {f: sum(pred[i][f] == UNPARSED for i in IDS) for f in FIELDS},
 213              'unavailable': sum(pred[i]['distance'] == UNAVAILABLE for i in IDS),
 214              'confusion': {f: Counter((key[i][f], pred[i][f]) for i in IDS) for f in FIELDS}}
 215  
 216  
 217  def preds(rows, ph):
 218      """{method: {id: prediction}}; a vision method needs one row per id, else it is missing."""
 219      out = {'Y': {i: y_pred(p) for i, p in ph.items()}}
 220      for m in VISION_METHODS:
 221          got = {x['id']: x for x in rows if x['method'] == m}
 222          if sorted(got) == IDS:
 223              out[m] = {i: parse(got[i].get('content')) for i in IDS}
 224      return out
 225  
 226  
 227  def verdict(mets):
 228      vis = [m for m in VISION_METHODS if m in mets]
 229      if not vis or 'Y' not in mets:
 230          return 'FAIL', 'a method is missing'
 231      best = max(mets[m]['acc']['room'] for m in vis)
 232      top = [m for m in vis if mets[m]['acc']['room'] == best]
 233      win = [m for m in top if best >= 0.80 and mets[m]['acc']['person'] >= mets['Y']['acc']['person']]
 234      return ('PASS' if win else 'FAIL'), (f'best room {best:.3f} by {top}; person {[round(mets[m]["acc"]["person"], 3) for m in top]}'
 235                                           f' vs Y {mets["Y"]["acc"]["person"]:.3f}; meets both: {win or "none"}')
 236  
 237  
 238  def validity(run, rows):
 239      probs = []
 240      for launch, _, methods in LAUNCHES:
 241          lr = [x for x in rows if x['launch'] == launch]
 242          if len(lr) != len(methods) * len(IDS):
 243              probs.append(f'launch {launch}: {len(lr)} of {len(methods) * len(IDS)} rows')
 244          if len({(x['pid'], x['pid_start']) for x in lr}) > 1:
 245              probs.append(f'launch {launch}: more than one server PID')
 246      errors = sum('error' in x for x in rows)
 247      probs += [f'{errors} request errors'] if errors else []
 248      probs += [f'exception: {run["exception"]}'] if run.get('exception') else []
 249      probs += [f'end check: {p}' for p in run.get('end_problems', [])]
 250      return ('INVALID' if probs else 'VALID'), probs
 251  
 252  
 253  # ---------------------------------------------------------------- report
 254  
 255  def confusion_text(c, rows_lab, cols_lab):
 256      w = max(12, *(len(s) + 1 for s in cols_lab))
 257      L = ['    key \\ pred'.ljust(16) + ''.join(s.rjust(w) for s in cols_lab)]
 258      return L + [f'    {r:<12}' + ''.join(str(c.get((r, s), 0)).rjust(w) for s in cols_lab) for r in rows_lab]
 259  
 260  
 261  def report(run, rows, ph, key, mets):
 262      L = [f'MIND-BV {run["utc"]}  code {run["code_sha256"][:12]}  base {run["git_head"]}',
 263           f'results: accuracy {run["validity"]} (temperature 0); timings NOT MEASUREMENTS (agent resident)',
 264           f'key {run["key"]["path"]} sha256 {run["key"]["sha256"]}', f'pins: {run["pins"]}', f'detector behind Y: '
 265           f'{run["detector"]}'] + [f'launch {l}: {" ".join(c)}' for l, c in run['commands'].items()] + \
 266          [f'launch {l}: exit code {e["exit_code"]} after SIGTERM, SIGKILL {e["killed"]}' +
 267           ('' if e['exit_code'] == 0 and not e['killed'] else '  FINDING (#145)') for l, e in run['exits'].items()]
 268      if run['problems']:
 269          L.append('validity problems: ' + '; '.join(run['problems']))
 270      L += ['', f'{"method":<6} {"room":>11} {"person":>11} {"distance":>11} {"all3":>6}  UNPARSED (room/person/dist)']
 271      for m, x in mets.items():
 272          c, n = x['correct'], x['n']
 273          L.append(f'{m:<6} ' + ' '.join(f'{c[f]:>2}/{n[f]} {x["acc"][f]:.3f}'.rjust(11) for f in FIELDS) +
 274                   f' {x["all3"]:>3}/61  {x["unparsed"]["room"]}/{x["unparsed"]["person"]}/{x["unparsed"]["distance"]}' +
 275                   (f'  (distance UNAVAILABLE {x["unavailable"]})' if x['unavailable'] else ''))
 276      L += ['', f'VERDICT: {run["verdict"][0]}  ({run["verdict"][1]})']
 277      for m, x in mets.items():
 278          L += ['', f'--- {m}'] + [f'  wrong {f}: {x["wrong"][f]}' for f in FIELDS]
 279          for f in FIELDS:
 280              cols = list(ALLOWED[f]) + [s for s in (UNPARSED, UNAVAILABLE) if any(k[1] == s for k in x['confusion'][f])]
 281              rws = [r for r in ALLOWED[f] if any(k[0] == r for k in x['confusion'][f])]
 282              L += [f'  confusion {f}' + (' (all 61)' if f == 'distance' else '')] + confusion_text(x['confusion'][f], rws, cols)
 283      L += ['', '--- prompt tokens per method (usage; min-max)']
 284      for m in VISION_METHODS:
 285          t = [x['usage']['prompt_tokens'] for x in rows if x['method'] == m and (x.get('usage') or {}).get('prompt_tokens')]
 286          L.append(f'  {m}: {min(t)}-{max(t)}' if t else f'  {m}: none')
 287      L += ['', '--- every reply (id, method, key, parsed, verbatim; * = field wrong)']
 288      for x in rows:
 289          p = parse(x.get('content'))
 290          k = key[x['id']]
 291          marks = ' '.join(f'{p[f]}{"*" if p[f] != k[f] else ""}' for f in FIELDS)
 292          L.append(f'{x["id"]:>2} {x["method"]:<5} key {k["room"]}|{k["person"]}|{k["distance"]}  got {marks}  '
 293                   f'{x.get("content")!r}' + (f'  ERROR {x["error"]}' if 'error' in x else ''))
 294      L += ['', '--- Y predictions (id: room|person|distance; classes)']
 295      L += [f'{i:>2} {"|".join(y_pred(ph[i])[f] for f in FIELDS)}  {classes(ph[i])}' for i in IDS]
 296      return '\n'.join(L) + '\n'
 297  
 298  
 299  def detector_info():
 300      m = json.loads(MANIFEST.read_text())
 301      dp = Path(detect_person.__file__)
 302      return {'model': m['yolo_files']['640'], 'input': '640x640 (quality1.py: detector.detect(frame, 640); plain resize)',
 303              'conf': f'> {detect_person.CONF}', 'iou_nms': detect_person.IOU,
 304              'detect_person_sha256': sha(dp.read_bytes()), 'detect_person_sha256_in_run': m['code_sha256']['detect_person.py']}
 305  
 306  
 307  def finish(out, run, rows, ph, key):
 308      run['end_problems'] = frame_problems(ph, key)
 309      run['validity'], run['problems'] = validity(run, rows)
 310      mets = {m: metrics(p, key) for m, p in preds(rows, ph).items()}
 311      run['verdict'] = verdict(mets)
 312      run['metrics'] = {m: {**x, 'confusion': {f: [[*k, v] for k, v in sorted(c.items())] for f, c in x['confusion'].items()}}
 313                        for m, x in mets.items()}
 314      (out / 'run.json').write_text(json.dumps(run, indent=1) + '\n')
 315      text = report(run, rows, ph, key, mets)
 316      (out / 'report.txt').write_text(text)
 317      files = sorted(p for p in out.rglob('*') if p.is_file() and p.name != 'artifacts.sha256')
 318      (out / 'artifacts.sha256').write_text(''.join(f'{sha(p.read_bytes())}  {p.relative_to(out)}\n' for p in files))
 319      return text
 320  
 321  
 322  def stop(proc):
 323      killed = False
 324      if proc.poll() is None:
 325          os.killpg(proc.pid, signal.SIGTERM)
 326          try:
 327              proc.wait(timeout=b0.TERM_WAIT_S)
 328          except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
 329              os.killpg(proc.pid, signal.SIGKILL)
 330              proc.wait()
 331              killed = True
 332      return {'exit_code': proc.returncode, 'killed': killed}
 333  
 334  
 335  def main(argv=None):
 336      a = argparse.ArgumentParser()
 337      a.add_argument('--key', required=True)
 338      args = a.parse_args(argv)
 339      key = load_key(args.key)
 340      frames = sorted(p.name for p in FRAMES_DIR.glob('*.jpg'))
 341      results_bytes = RESULTS.read_bytes()
 342      ph = photos(json.loads(results_bytes), frames)
 343      bad = frame_problems(ph, key)
 344      bad += [f'{p}: SHA-256 != pinned {h[:12]}' for p, h in bc.PINS.items() if not Path(p).is_file() or cr.sha256(p) != h]
 345      bad += vis3d.env_problems()
 346      if bad:
 347          raise SystemExit('refused: ' + '; '.join(bad))
 348      if not b0.repro.port_free():
 349          raise SystemExit('refused: port 8080 in use (never stops a server it did not start)')
 350      utc = time.strftime('%Y%m%dT%H%M%SZ', time.gmtime())
 351      out = OUT_ROOT / utc
 352      out.mkdir(parents=True)  # a new folder; an existing one raises
 353      run = {'utc': utc, 'code_sha256': sha(Path(__file__).read_bytes()),
 354             'imports_sha256': {m.__name__: sha(Path(m.__file__).read_bytes()) for m in (bc, b0, mem1, vis3d)},
 355             'git_head': bc.run_out('git', '-C', str(HERE), 'rev-parse', 'HEAD').strip(), 'pins': bc.PINS,
 356             'key': {'path': str(Path(args.key).resolve()), 'sha256': KEY_SHA256},
 357             'results_sha256': sha(results_bytes), 'detector': detector_info(),
 358             'commands': {l: command(t) for l, t, _ in LAUNCHES}, 'exits': {}, 'timings': 'NOT MEASUREMENTS'}
 359      (out / 'run.json').write_text(json.dumps(run, indent=1) + '\n')
 360      rows, exc = [], None
 361      with open(out / 'requests.jsonl', 'x') as rq:
 362          def emit(row):
 363              rq.write(json.dumps(row) + '\n')
 364              rq.flush()
 365          for launch, tokens, methods in LAUNCHES:
 366              proc = None
 367              with open(out / f'server_{launch}.log', 'x') as log:
 368                  try:
 369                      proc = subprocess.Popen(run['commands'][launch], stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL, stdout=log,
 370                                              stderr=subprocess.STDOUT, start_new_session=True)
 371                      t0 = time.monotonic()
 372                      while not cr.healthy():
 373                          if proc.poll() is not None or time.monotonic() - t0 > b0.LOAD_TIMEOUT_S:
 374                              raise RuntimeError(f'llama-server not healthy (exit {proc.poll()})')
 375                          time.sleep(0.1)
 376                      post = lambda path, b: cr.post(path, b, timeout=b0.REQUEST_TIMEOUT_S)  # noqa: E731
 377                      for j, m in enumerate(methods):
 378                          rows += run_method(post, launch, m, ph, proc.pid, emit, first_block=j == 0)
 379                  except BaseException as e:  # recorded; the report is still written, then a stop re-raises
 380                      exc = e
 381                      run['exception'] = f'launch {launch}: {type(e).__name__}: {e}'
 382                  finally:
 383                      if proc is not None:
 384                          run['exits'][launch] = stop(proc)
 385              if exc is not None:
 386                  break
 387      print(finish(out, run, rows, ph, key) + f'\n{out}')
 388      if exc is not None and not isinstance(exc, Exception):
 389          raise exc
 390  
 391  
 392  if __name__ == '__main__':
 393      main()
END
BEGIN benchmark/gemma_vision/bv_compare/test_bv.py (175 lines)
   1  """Offline tests for bv.py with a fake server: Y rules (room priority, largest person box, one-box photos), the
   2  detector line, parsing incl. UNPARSED, metrics, the PASS rule, key custody and the run path. No server, no phone."""
   3  import json
   4  import sys
   5  import tempfile
   6  import unittest
   7  from pathlib import Path
   8  
   9  sys.dont_write_bytecode = True
  10  sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent))
  11  import bv  # noqa: E402
  12  
  13  bv.PAUSE_S = 0
  14  FRAMES = sorted(p.name for p in bv.FRAMES_DIR.glob('*.jpg'))
  15  PH = bv.photos(json.loads(bv.RESULTS.read_text()), FRAMES)
  16  
  17  
  18  def det(cls, box=(0, 0, 10, 10), dist='far', pos='center'):
  19      return {'class_name': cls, 'box_xyxy': list(box), 'distance': dist, 'position': pos}
  20  
  21  
  22  def key_from(pred_fn):
  23      return {i: {'frame': FRAMES[i - 1], **pred_fn(i)} for i in bv.IDS}
  24  
  25  
  26  class FakeServer:
  27      def __init__(self, reply):
  28          self.reply, self.bodies = reply, []
  29  
  30      def __call__(self, path, body):
  31          assert path == '/v1/chat/completions'
  32          self.bodies.append(body)
  33          return {'choices': [{'message': {'content': self.reply(body)}, 'finish_reason': 'stop'}],
  34                  'usage': {'prompt_tokens': 100}}
  35  
  36  
  37  class T(unittest.TestCase):
  38      def test_room_priority(self):
  39          y = lambda *c: bv.y_pred({'detections': [det(x) for x in c]})['room']  # noqa: E731
  40          self.assertEqual(y('couch', 'toilet', 'bed'), 'bathroom')
  41          self.assertEqual(y('couch', 'oven', 'bed'), 'bedroom')
  42          self.assertEqual(y('tv', 'microwave'), 'kitchen')
  43          self.assertEqual(y('dining table', 'cup'), 'living room')
  44          self.assertEqual(y('cup', 'chair'), 'unknown')
  45          self.assertEqual(bv.y_pred({'detections': None, 'classes': ['toilet']})['room'], 'bathroom')
  46  
  47      def test_person_largest_box(self):
  48          p = {'detections': [det('person', (0, 0, 10, 10), 'far'), det('person', (0, 0, 100, 100), 'very close'),
  49                              det('couch', (0, 0, 500, 500), 'medium')]}
  50          self.assertEqual(bv.y_pred(p), {'room': 'living room', 'person': 'yes', 'distance': 'close'})
  51          p['detections'][1]['distance'] = 'medium'
  52          self.assertEqual(bv.y_pred(p)['distance'], 'medium')
  53          self.assertEqual(bv.y_pred({'detections': [det('cup')]}), {'room': 'unknown', 'person': 'no', 'distance': 'none'})
  54          one = {'detections': None, 'classes': ['person']}
  55          self.assertEqual(bv.y_pred(one), {'room': 'unknown', 'person': 'yes', 'distance': bv.UNAVAILABLE})
  56  
  57      def test_real_photos(self):
  58          self.assertEqual(bv.y_pred(PH[10])['distance'], bv.UNAVAILABLE)  # one-box person photo
  59          self.assertEqual(bv.y_pred(PH[33])['room'], 'bathroom')
  60          self.assertEqual(bv.detector_line(PH[10]), "Objects the robot's detector found: person (position unknown, "
  61                                                     "distance unknown)")
  62          self.assertTrue(bv.detector_line(PH[1]).startswith("Objects the robot's detector found: cup (center, far), "
  63                                                             "chair (right, medium), "))
  64          self.assertEqual(bv.detector_line({'detections': [], 'classes': []}), "Objects the robot's detector found: none")
  65  
  66      def test_prompt(self):
  67          v, vy = bv.prompt('V70', PH[1]).splitlines(), bv.prompt('VY70', PH[1]).splitlines()
  68          self.assertEqual(len(v), 6)
  69          self.assertEqual(vy[:1] + vy[2:], v)
  70          self.assertTrue(vy[1].startswith("Objects the robot's detector found: ") and vy[2].startswith('Answer'))
  71          b = bv.body('V140', PH[1], 'AAAA')
  72          self.assertEqual((b['temperature'], b['top_k'], b['seed'], b['max_tokens'], b['chat_template_kwargs']),
  73                           (0, 1, 0, 32, {'enable_thinking': False}))
  74          self.assertEqual(b['messages'][0]['content'][0]['image_url']['url'], 'data:image/jpeg;base64,AAAA')
  75  
  76      def test_command(self):
  77          for t in (70, 140):
  78              c = bv.command(t)
  79              base = bv.mem1.command(2048)
  80              self.assertEqual(c[:len(base)], [bv.bc.SERVER if x == bv.mem1.SERVER else x for x in base])
  81              self.assertEqual(c[len(base):], ['--mmproj', bv.vis3d.MMPROJ, '--image-min-tokens', str(t),
  82                                               '--image-max-tokens', str(t)])
  83              self.assertNotIn('--slot-save-path', c)
  84  
  85      def test_parse(self):
  86          ok = {'room': 'living room', 'person': 'yes', 'distance': 'close'}
  87          self.assertEqual(bv.parse('Room: Living Room\nPerson: Yes\nDistance: close'), ok)
  88          self.assertEqual(bv.parse('**Room:** living room.\n**Person:** yes\nDistance: close (under 1 m)'), ok)
  89          self.assertEqual(bv.parse('Room: lounge\nPerson: maybe\n'),
  90                           {'room': 'UNPARSED', 'person': 'UNPARSED', 'distance': 'UNPARSED'})
  91          self.assertEqual(bv.parse('Room: kitchen\nRoom: bedroom\nPerson: no\nDistance: none')['room'], 'UNPARSED')
  92          self.assertEqual(bv.parse('Room: close (under 1 m)')['room'], 'UNPARSED')
  93          self.assertEqual(bv.parse(None)['person'], 'UNPARSED')
  94  
  95      def test_metrics(self):
  96          key = key_from(lambda i: {'room': 'kitchen', 'person': 'yes' if i <= 10 else 'no',
  97                                    'distance': 'far' if i <= 10 else 'none'})
  98          pred = {i: dict(key[i]) for i in bv.IDS}
  99          pred[1]['person'] = 'no'              # person wrong -> distance wrong too
 100          pred[2]['distance'] = 'close'         # bucket wrong
 101          pred[3]['room'] = bv.UNPARSED
 102          pred[20]['distance'] = 'far'          # distance on a no-person photo: not in distance accuracy, not all3
 103          m = bv.metrics(pred, key)
 104          self.assertEqual(m['n'], {'room': 61, 'person': 61, 'distance': 10})
 105          self.assertEqual(m['correct'], {'room': 60, 'person': 60, 'distance': 8})
 106          self.assertEqual(m['wrong'], {'room': [3], 'person': [1], 'distance': [1, 2]})
 107          self.assertEqual(m['all3'], 61 - 4)
 108          self.assertEqual(m['unparsed']['room'], 1)
 109          self.assertEqual(m['confusion']['room'][('kitchen', bv.UNPARSED)], 1)
 110  
 111      def test_verdict(self):
 112          def mets(room, person):
 113              return {'acc': {'room': room, 'person': person, 'distance': 0}}
 114          y = mets(0.5, 0.9)
 115          self.assertEqual(bv.verdict({'Y': y, 'V70': mets(0.85, 0.9), 'V140': mets(0.7, 1), 'VY70': mets(0.8, 1)})[0],
 116                           'PASS')
 117          self.assertEqual(bv.verdict({'Y': y, 'V70': mets(0.85, 0.8), 'V140': mets(0.7, 1), 'VY70': mets(0.8, 1)})[0],
 118                           'FAIL')  # the best room method loses on person
 119          self.assertEqual(bv.verdict({'Y': y, 'V70': mets(0.79, 1), 'V140': mets(0.7, 1), 'VY70': mets(0.6, 1)})[0],
 120                           'FAIL')
 121          self.assertEqual(bv.verdict({'Y': y, 'V70': mets(0.9, 0.8), 'V140': mets(0.9, 0.95), 'VY70': mets(0.6, 1)})[0],
 122                           'PASS')  # tie on room: one of the tied methods meets both
 123  
 124      def test_custody(self):
 125          with self.assertRaises(SystemExit):
 126              bv.load_key(Path(__file__))  # inside the repo: refused before reading
 127          with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as d:
 128              k = Path(d) / 'k.json'
 129              k.write_text(json.dumps({str(i): {} for i in bv.IDS}))
 130              with self.assertRaises(SystemExit):
 131                  bv.load_key(k)  # hash differs from the pin
 132              self.assertEqual(sorted(bv.load_key(k, bv.sha(k.read_bytes()))), bv.IDS)
 133  
 134      def test_frames(self):
 135          key = key_from(lambda i: {})
 136          self.assertEqual(bv.frame_problems(PH, key), [])
 137          key[5]['frame'] = FRAMES[61]
 138          self.assertEqual(len(bv.frame_problems(PH, key)), 1)
 139  
 140      def test_run_path(self):
 141          key = key_from(lambda i: {'room': 'living room', 'person': 'yes' if i in (10, 11) else 'no',
 142                                    'distance': 'far' if i in (10, 11) else 'none'})
 143          url = {'data:image/jpeg;base64,' + bv.base64.b64encode((bv.FRAMES_DIR / FRAMES[i - 1]).read_bytes()).decode(): i
 144                 for i in bv.IDS}
 145  
 146          def reply(b):  # VY70 answers as the key; V70/V140 omit the distance line
 147              k = key[url[b['messages'][0]['content'][0]['image_url']['url']]]
 148              if 'detector' in b['messages'][0]['content'][1]['text']:
 149                  return f'Room: {k["room"]}\nPerson: {k["person"]}\nDistance: {k["distance"]}'
 150              return 'Room: kitchen\nPerson: no'
 151          fake = FakeServer(reply)
 152          rows = []
 153          for launch, _, methods in bv.LAUNCHES:
 154              for j, m in enumerate(methods):
 155                  rows += bv.run_method(fake, launch, m, PH, 123, lambda r: None, first_block=j == 0)
 156          self.assertEqual(len(fake.bodies), 183)
 157          self.assertEqual([x['block'] for x in rows[:62:30]], [0, 0, 1])  # 31 + 30 per method
 158          with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as d:
 159              run = {'utc': 'T', 'code_sha256': 'c' * 64, 'git_head': 'h', 'key': {'path': 'k', 'sha256': 's'},
 160                     'pins': {}, 'detector': {}, 'commands': {'A': ['x'], 'B': ['y']},
 161                     'exits': {'A': {'exit_code': 0, 'killed': False}, 'B': {'exit_code': -11, 'killed': False}}}
 162              text = bv.finish(Path(d), run, rows, PH, key)
 163              out = json.loads((Path(d) / 'run.json').read_text())
 164              self.assertEqual(out['validity'], 'VALID')
 165              self.assertEqual(out['metrics']['VY70']['all3'], 61)
 166              self.assertEqual(out['metrics']['V70']['unparsed']['distance'], 61)
 167              self.assertEqual(out['verdict'][0], 'PASS')
 168              self.assertIn('FINDING (#145)', text)
 169              self.assertEqual(len((Path(d) / 'artifacts.sha256').read_text().splitlines()), 2)
 170              rows[5]['error'] = 'x'
 171              self.assertEqual(bv.validity(run, rows[:-1])[0], 'INVALID')
 172  
 173  
 174  if __name__ == '__main__':
 175      unittest.main()

BEGIN benchmark/gemma_vision/bv_compare/README.md (23 lines)
   1  # MIND-BV — Gemma vision vs YOLO: room, person, distance
   2  
   3  Research only. On the robot's photos (M2-Q1 frames, `relate_anything/quality1/runs/20261017T213604Z/frames/`, ids
   4  1–60 and 66), how well does Gemma 4 E2B vision name the room, see a person and judge the person's distance, compared
   5  with the robot's YOLO detections plus simple rules? Accuracy is deterministic (temperature 0; agent resident
   6  allowed); timings NOT MEASUREMENTS.
   7  
   8  - Methods: **Y** (results.json detections + room/person/distance rules, no new YOLO run), **V70** / **V140** (Gemma
   9    vision, 70 / 140 image tokens), **VY70** (V70 plus a line listing Y's detections). Rules, prompt, parsing,
  10    metrics and the PASS rule are in the docstring of `bv.py` and were fixed before the first Gemma call.
  11  - Owner decisions 2026-11-03: the key has 40 person photos (not 44); photos with one detection keep no box in
  12    results.json, so Y's distance there is UNAVAILABLE (scored wrong) and VY70 lists them as
  13    `<class> (position unknown, distance unknown)`.
  14  - Server: BC's b11716 command (`mem1.command(2048)`, b11716 `llama-server`, BC's pins) + `--mmproj` Q8_0 +
  15    `--image-min-tokens`/`--image-max-tokens` 70 (launch A: V70, VY70) or 140 (launch B: V140); no slot path, no MTP.
  16  - Key custody: `bv_key.json` stays outside the repo (refused inside it); SHA-256 pinned in `bv.py`
  17    (`456f633b…`). Every frame's SHA-256 is checked against results.json before any call and again at the end.
  18  - Run: `python bv.py --key /termux-home/mind_bv_key/<UTC>/bv_key.json` (Termux python, also from proot Debian).
  19    Writes `~/robot_runs/mind_bv/<UTC>/` (run.json, requests.jsonl, server_A.log, server_B.log, report.txt,
  20    artifacts.sha256).
  21  - Offline tests: `python -B test_bv.py` (fake server; no phone).
  22  - Known limitation carried over from B0: an interrupt or a server-stop error can skip report.txt and
  23    artifacts.sha256 (completed rows stay in requests.jsonl).
END

## E. Context (unchanged, numbered with original line numbers)
BEGIN benchmark/mind_picker/bc_build/bc.py (365 lines)
   1  #!/data/data/com.termux/files/usr/bin/python
   2  """MIND-BC: can the vision build (b11716 + Q8 projector, ~/llama.cpp-vis) replace the robot build ("b1609", upstream
   3  tag b10194, ~/llama.cpp-b1609-dotprod, #119) for the picker? Research only: no motors, camera, YOLO, M2, main.py or
   4  run_mission.py; no MTP. Counters and accuracy are deterministic (temperature 0); timings NOT MEASUREMENTS; RAM
   5  INDICATIVE (an agent may be resident).
   6  
   7  Imports B0 (b0_cache), B0b (b0b_slots), B2 (b2_rules) and VIS-3D (gemma_vision/vis3d) unchanged; only the server
   8  command differs: mem1.command(2048) (setup_q4 flags) with the b11716 llama-server, plus --slot-save-path <run>/slots
   9  and VIS-3D's projector flags (--mmproj Q8_0, --image-min-tokens 70 --image-max-tokens 70). Same model file and pin.
  10  provenance.txt (read-only, both trees): source commit and tag, CMakeCache CPU flags, SHA-256 of llama-server and
  11  libggml-cpu, --help presence of the flags in HELP_FLAGS and the --cache-ram / --kv-unified-per-slot help text.
  12  One launch, in order:
  13    K1 B0's sequence (b0.run_sequence, b0_set.json): B0's C1-C5, plus a row diff (DIFF_FIELDS) vs the B0 run of record.
  14    K2 B0b's L1 sequence (b0b.run_sequence 'L1'): B0b's K1 (restore ok, R7 cache_n >= min(LCP restored, n - 1)); slot
  15       file size.
  16    K3 B2 V3 on B2's TEST set (b2_rules/set/b2_test.json, SHA-256 pinned; custody ended), FWD then REV, 60 s pause
  17       before each block (b2.run_blocks): B2's PASS rule (b1.PASS: acceptable >= 0.80 and strict >= 0.65 in both
  18       orders, flips <= 4); items whose top differs from the B2 run of record.
  19    K4 one image request, VIS-3D frame 1 (vis3d.image_payload: prompt, temperature 0, top_k 1, seed 0, max_tokens 96)
  20       plus chat_template_kwargs enable_thinking false (the per-request form of VIS-3D D's --reasoning off, which the
  21       fixed server flags do not include): PASS when the reply is non-empty.
  22    K5 one server PID over all rows, all rows present, 0 request errors, no exception. The server exit code after
  23       SIGTERM is reported; nonzero is a FINDING (#145), not part of K5 (B0's C5 still requires 0, as in B0).
  24  RAM (llama-server Pss, VmRSS, VmHWM; MemAvailable) after load and at the end, against B0b L0 (b1609).
  25  Exceptions keep the rows collected so far and fail the criteria they affect (B0's interrupt/cleanup limitation is
  26  carried over).
  27  
  28  Usage: bc.py   (Termux python; from proot Debian or native Termux)
  29  Writes ~/robot_runs/mind_bc/<UTC>/: run.json, requests.jsonl, provenance.txt, server.log, slots/, report.txt,
  30  artifacts.sha256.
  31  """

  52  SERVER = vis3d.SERVER
  53  LIBCPU = f'{vis3d.BIN}/libggml-cpu.so.0.27.0'
  54  PINS = {SERVER: vis3d.BUILD_SHA256['llama-server'], LIBCPU: vis3d.BUILD_SHA256['libggml-cpu.so.0.27.0'],
  55          mem1.MODEL: mem1.PINNED[mem1.MODEL], vis3d.MMPROJ: vis3d.SHA256[vis3d.MMPROJ]}

  59  B0B_L1 = HERE.parent / 'b0b_slots' / 'runs' / '20261102T123830Z_L1' / 'run.json'
  60  B2_RECORD = HERE.parent / 'b2_rules' / 'runs' / '20261102T143251Z' / 'requests.jsonl'

  67  HELP_FLAGS = ('--swa-full', '--cache-ram', '--parallel', '--slot-save-path', '--kv-unified', '--mmproj',
  68                '--image-min-tokens', '--image-max-tokens')
  69  CMAKE = ('CMAKE_BUILD_TYPE', 'GGML_NATIVE', 'GGML_CPU_ARM_ARCH', 'HAVE_DOTPROD', 'CMAKE_C_FLAGS', 'CMAKE_CXX_FLAGS',
  70           'BUILD_SHARED_LIBS', 'GGML_BACKEND_DL', 'GGML_CPU_ALL_VARIANTS')
  71  N_ROWS = {'K1': len(b0.SEQUENCE) + len(b0.FRESH), 'K2': b0b.n_rows('L1'), 'K4': 1}

  74  def command(slot_dir):
  75      cmd = mem1.command(CTX)
  76      cmd[cmd.index(mem1.SERVER)] = SERVER
  77      return cmd + ['--slot-save-path', str(slot_dir), *vis3d.VISION]

  80  def run_out(*cmd):
  81      return subprocess.run(cmd, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=60).stdout
  82  
  83  
  84  def help_has(text, flag):
  85      return flag in text.split() or flag + ',' in text
  86  
  87  
  88  def provenance():
  89      """Read-only facts of both trees, as text."""
  90      L = []
  91      for name, t in TREES.items():
  92          cache = (t / 'build' / 'CMakeCache.txt').read_text().splitlines()
  93          keys = {k: next((l.split('=', 1)[1] for l in cache if l.split(':', 1)[0] == k), 'absent') for k in CMAKE}
  94          i8mm = [l for l in cache if 'i8mm' in l.lower() and not l.startswith('//')]
  95          server = t / 'build' / 'bin' / 'llama-server'
  96          helptext = run_out(str(server), '--help')
  97          L += [f'== {name}: {t}', f'source commit {run_out("git", "-C", str(t), "rev-parse", "HEAD").strip()}  '
  98                f'describe {run_out("git", "-C", str(t), "describe", "--tags").strip()}  '
  99                f'uncommitted {len(run_out("git", "-C", str(t), "status", "--porcelain").splitlines())} lines',
 100                *(f'  {k} = {v}' for k, v in keys.items()), f'  i8mm entries: {i8mm or "none"}',
 101                *(f'  sha256 {sha(p.read_bytes())}  {p.name}'
 102                  for p in [server, *sorted((t / 'build' / 'bin').glob('libggml-cpu*.so*'))] if p.is_file()
 103                  and not p.is_symlink()),
 104                '  --help: ' + ' '.join(f'{f}={"yes" if help_has(helptext, f) else "NO"}' for f in HELP_FLAGS)]
 105          lines = helptext.splitlines()
 106          for flag in ('--cache-ram', '--kv-unified-per-slot'):
 107              i = next((j for j, l in enumerate(lines) if flag in l), None)
 108              L += [f'  help {flag}: ' + (' '.join(x.strip() for x in lines[i:i + 3]) if i is not None else 'absent')]
 109      return '\n'.join(L) + '\n'

 112  def tagged(emit, phase):
 113      return lambda row: emit({'phase': phase, **row})
 114  
 115  
 116  def k1(post, s, pid, emit):
 117      return b0.run_sequence(post, s, pid, tagged(emit, 'K1'))
 118  
 119  
 120  def k2(post, s, pid, emit):
 121      return b0b.run_sequence(post, s, 'L1', pid, tagged(emit, 'K2'))
 122  
 123  
 124  def k3(post, test, pid, emit):
 125      """B2's TEST preflight and the two V3 blocks (b2.run_blocks pauses 60 s before each). Returns (rows, info)."""
 126      rows, info = [], {'problems': []}
 127      try:
 128          prompts = b2.preflight(post, test, K3_BLOCKS, info, 'TEST')
 129          if info['problems']:
 130              info['stopped'] = 'STOP before K3 inference: ' + '; '.join(info['problems'])
 131              return rows, info
 132          b2.run_blocks(post, test, K3_BLOCKS, prompts, pid, tagged(emit, 'K3'), rows, info, False)
 133      except Exception as e:
 134          info['exception'] = f'{type(e).__name__}: {e}'
 135          info['stopped'] = f'exception after {len(rows)} K3 requests: {info["exception"]}'
 136      return rows, info
 137  
 138  
 139  def k4(post, pid, emit, jpeg=None):
 140      body = {**vis3d.image_payload(FRAME, jpeg), 'chat_template_kwargs': {'enable_thinking': False}}
 141      row = {'phase': 'K4', 'id': FRAME, 'pid': pid, 'pid_start': b0.pid_start(pid)}
 142      t0 = time.perf_counter()
 143      try:
 144          res = post('/v1/chat/completions', body)
 145          msg = res['choices'][0]['message']
 146          row.update(content=msg.get('content'), reasoning_content=msg.get('reasoning_content'),
 147                     finish_reason=res['choices'][0].get('finish_reason'), usage=res.get('usage'),
 148                     timings=res.get('timings'))
 149      except Exception as e:  # recorded, K4 and K5 fail
 150          row['error'] = f'{type(e).__name__}: {e}'
 151      row['wall_s'] = round(time.perf_counter() - t0, 3)
 152      emit(row)
 153      return [row], {k: v for k, v in body.items() if k != 'messages'}
 154  
 155  
 156  def diff_rows(rows, ref, fields=DIFF_FIELDS):
 157      """[(id, {field: (record, now)})] for every record row that is missing or differs in a field."""
 158      got = {x['id']: x for x in rows}
 159      out = []
 160      for r in ref:
 161          x = got.get(r['id'])
 162          d = {f: (r.get(f), None if x is None else x.get(f)) for f in fields if x is None or x.get(f) != r.get(f)}
 163          if d:
 164              out.append((r['id'], d))
 165      return out
 166  
 167  
 168  def agreement(rows, ref):
 169      """[(order, id, record top, now top)] for TEST V3 items whose choice differs (or is missing) from the record."""
 170      now = {(x['order'], x['id']): x.get('top') for x in rows}
 171      return [(o, i, t, now.get((o, i))) for (o, i), t in
 172              sorted(((r['order'], r['id']), r['top']) for r in ref if r.get('set') == 'TEST' and r['variant'] == 'V3')
 173              if now.get((o, i)) != t]
 174  
 175  
 176  def criteria(ph, run, test, refs):
 177      """[(name, PASS|FAIL|INFO|FINDING, text)] from the recorded rows alone. ph[phase] = (rows, info)."""
 178      code, killed, exc = run.get('exit_code'), run.get('killed'), run.get('exception')
 179      rows1, info1 = ph.get('K1', ([], {}))
 180      out = [(f'K1 {c}', v, t) for c, v, t in b0.criteria(rows1, info1, code, killed, exc)]
 181      d = diff_rows(rows1, refs['b0'])
 182      out.append(('K1 diff', 'INFO', f'{len(d)} of {len(refs["b0"])} rows differ from the B0 run of record in '
 183                  f'{DIFF_FIELDS}: ' + ('; '.join(f'{i} {v}' for i, v in d) or 'none')))
 184      rows2, info2 = ph.get('K2', ([], {}))
 185      c = next(x for x in b0b.criteria('L1', rows2, info2, code, killed, exc) if x[0] == 'K1')
 186      out.append(('K2', c[1], c[2] + f'; slot file {run.get("slot_file_bytes")} bytes (b1609 B0b L1: '
 187                  f'{refs["b0b_l1_bytes"]})'))
 188      rows3, info3 = ph.get('K3', ([], {}))
 189      F, R, arm = b2.block_metrics(rows3, test, 'V3')
 190      full3 = len(rows3) == 2 * len(test['items']) and not any('error' in x for x in rows3) and not info3.get('stopped')
 191      out.append(('K3', arm['verdict'] if full3 else 'FAIL', f'strict {F["strict"]:.3f}/{R["strict"]:.3f}, acceptable '
 192                  f'{F["acceptable"]:.3f}/{R["acceptable"]:.3f} (FWD/REV), flips {len(arm["flips"])} {arm["flips"]}; '
 193                  f'{len(rows3)}/{2 * len(test["items"])} rows'))
 194      a = agreement(rows3, refs['b2'])
 195      out.append(('K3 agreement', 'INFO', f'{len(a)} of {2 * len(test["items"])} items differ from the B2 run of '
 196                  f'record (order, id, b1609, b11716): {a or "none"}'))
 197      rows4 = ph.get('K4', ([], {}))[0]
 198      ok4 = len(rows4) == 1 and 'error' not in rows4[0] and bool((rows4[0].get('content') or '').strip())
 199      out.append(('K4', 'PASS' if ok4 else 'FAIL', f'reply {rows4[0].get("content") if rows4 else None!r}'))
 200      every = [x for p in ('K1', 'K2', 'K3', 'K4') for x in ph.get(p, ([], {}))[0]]
 201      pids = {(x.get('pid'), x.get('pid_start')) for x in every}
 202      errors = sum('error' in x for x in every)
 203      counts = {p: len(ph.get(p, ([], {}))[0]) for p in ('K1', 'K2', 'K3', 'K4')}
 204      want = {**N_ROWS, 'K3': 2 * len(test['items'])}
 205      stops = [f'{p}: {ph[p][1].get("stopped")}' for p in ph if ph[p][1].get('stopped')]
 206      ok5 = len(pids) == 1 and errors == 0 and counts == {p: want[p] for p in counts} and not exc and not stops
 207      out.append(('K5', 'PASS' if ok5 else 'FAIL', f'PIDs {sorted(pids, key=str)}; rows {counts} of {want}; '
 208                  f'{errors} errors; exception {exc}; stops {stops or "none"}'))
 209      out.append(('exit', 'INFO' if code == 0 and not killed else 'FINDING',
 210                  f'server exit code {code} after SIGTERM, SIGKILL {killed}'))
 211      return out
 212  
 213  
 214  def ram(pid):
 215      return b0b.ram(pid)
 216  
 217  
 218  def report(run, ph, crit, refs):
 219      L = [f'MIND-BC {run["utc"]}  code {run["code_sha256"][:12]}  base {run["git_head"]}',
 220           f'server: {" ".join(run["command"])}', f'pins: {run["pins"]}',
 221           f'label: counters and accuracy {run["validity"]}; timings NOT MEASUREMENTS; RAM INDICATIVE', '',
 222           '--- provenance (read-only) ---', run['provenance'].rstrip(), '']
 223      if run.get('exception'):
 224          L.append(f'EXCEPTION (runner): {run["exception"]}')
 225      ref0 = {x['id']: x for x in refs['b0']}
 226      for p in ('K1', 'K2'):
 227          rows, info = ph.get(p, ([], {}))
 228          L += [f'--- {p} rows (now | B0 run of record, b1609) ---',
 229                'id        kind      n    prompt_n cache_n  top      | n    prompt_n cache_n  top']
 230          for x in rows:
 231              r = ref0.get(x['id'], {})
 232              L.append(f'{x["id"]:<9} {x["kind"]:<9} {x["prompt_tokens"]:<4} {str(x.get("prompt_n")):<8} '
 233                       f'{str(x.get("cache_n")):<8} {str(x.get("top")):<8} | {str(r.get("prompt_tokens")):<4} '
 234                       f'{str(r.get("prompt_n")):<8} {str(r.get("cache_n")):<8} {r.get("top")}' +
 235                       (f'  ERROR {x["error"]}' if 'error' in x else ''))
 236          L += [f'  reply R4 {info.get("reply")!r}; R6 {info.get("r6_reply")!r}'] + \
 237               [f'  slot {a["action"]}: {a["response"]}' for a in info.get('slot_actions', [])]
 238          if info.get('stopped'):
 239              L.append(f'  STOPPED: {info["stopped"]}')
 240      rows3, info3 = ph.get('K3', ([], {}))
 241      L += ['', '--- K3 (B2 V3, TEST) ---', f'prompt tokens {info3.get("prompt_tokens")}; name ids '
 242            f'{info3.get("name_ids")}'] + [f'block {x["block"]}: {x["duration_s"]} s*' for x in info3.get('blocks', [])]
 243      F, R, arm = b2.block_metrics([x for x in refs['b2'] if x.get('set') == 'TEST'], refs['test'], 'V3')
 244      L.append(f'B2 run of record (b1609): strict {F["strict"]:.3f}/{R["strict"]:.3f}, acceptable '
 245               f'{F["acceptable"]:.3f}/{R["acceptable"]:.3f}, flips {len(arm["flips"])} {arm["flips"]} -> {arm["verdict"]}')
 246      if info3.get('stopped'):
 247          L.append(f'STOPPED: {info3["stopped"]}')
 248      rows4, body4 = ph.get('K4', ([], {}))
 249      L += ['', '--- K4 (image, VIS-3D frame 1) ---', f'frame {FRAME}; settings {body4}']
 250      for x in rows4:
 251          L += [f'reply (verbatim): {x.get("content")!r}', f'reasoning_content: {x.get("reasoning_content")!r}; finish '
 252                f'{x.get("finish_reason")}; usage {x.get("usage")}; wall {x.get("wall_s")} s*'] + \
 253               ([f'ERROR {x["error"]}'] if 'error' in x else [])
 254      L.append(f'VIS-3D D reply for this frame (server --reasoning off, ctx 4096): {refs["vis3d_reply"]!r}')
 255      L += ['', 'RAM (INDICATIVE, kB): now b11716 + projector | b1609 B0b L0']
 256      for when in ('after_load', 'end'):
 257          a, b = run.get('ram', {}).get(when, {}), refs['b0b_l0_ram'].get(when, {})
 258          L.append(f'  {when}: ' + '; '.join(f'{k} {a.get(k)} | {b.get(k)}' for k in ('Pss', 'VmRSS', 'VmHWM',
 259                                                                                       'MemAvailable')))
 260      L += ['* timings NOT MEASUREMENTS (agent resident; no thermal or core control)', '', 'criteria:'] + \
 261           [f'  {c:<13} {v:<7} {t}' for c, v, t in crit]
 262      return '\n'.join(L) + '\n'
 263  
 264  
 265  def references():
 266      test, _ = b2.load_set(TEST_SET, b2.TEST_SHA256, custody=False)
 267      test['tag'] = 'TEST'
 268      d = [json.loads(l) for l in VIS3D_D.read_text().splitlines()]
 269      return {'b0': [json.loads(l) for l in B0_RECORD.read_text().splitlines()],
 270              'b2': [json.loads(l) for l in B2_RECORD.read_text().splitlines()], 'test': test,
 271              'b0b_l0_ram': json.loads(B0B_L0.read_text())['ram'],
 272              'b0b_l1_bytes': json.loads(B0B_L1.read_text()).get('slot_file_bytes'),
 273              'vis3d_reply': next((x.get('reply') for x in d if x.get('prompt_id') == FRAME), None)}
 274  
 275  
 276  def finish(out, run, ph, refs):
 277      s_end = sha(b0.SET.read_bytes()), sha(TEST_SET.read_bytes())
 278      run['set_sha256_end'] = {'b0': s_end[0], 'test': s_end[1]}
 279      run['criteria'] = crit = criteria(ph, run, refs['test'], refs)
 280      ok = dict((c, v) for c, v, _ in crit)['K5'] == 'PASS' and s_end == (b0b.SET_SHA256, b2.TEST_SHA256)
 281      run['validity'] = 'VALID' if ok else 'INVALID'
 282      run['info'] = {p: {k: v for k, v in i.items() if k != 'test'} for p, (_, i) in ph.items()}
 283      (out / 'run.json').write_text(json.dumps(run, indent=1) + '\n')
 284      text = report(run, ph, crit, refs)
 285      (out / 'report.txt').write_text(text)
 286      files = sorted(p for p in out.rglob('*') if p.is_file() and p.name != 'artifacts.sha256')
 287      (out / 'artifacts.sha256').write_text(''.join(f'{sha(p.read_bytes())}  {p.relative_to(out)}\n' for p in files))
 288      return text
 289  
 290  
 291  def main():
 292      s_bytes = b0.SET.read_bytes()
 293      if sha(s_bytes) != b0b.SET_SHA256:
 294          raise SystemExit(f'refused: {b0.SET} SHA-256 {sha(s_bytes)[:12]} != {b0b.SET_SHA256[:12]}')
 295      s = json.loads(s_bytes)
 296      refs = references()  # B2's TEST set: hash checked by load_set
 297      bad = [f'{p}: SHA-256 != pinned {h[:12]}' for p, h in PINS.items() if not Path(p).is_file() or cr.sha256(p) != h]
 298      bad += vis3d.frame_problems() + vis3d.env_problems()
 299      if bad:
 300          raise SystemExit('refused: ' + '; '.join(bad))
 301      if not b0.repro.port_free():
 302          raise SystemExit('refused: port 8080 in use (never stops a server it did not start)')
 303      utc = time.strftime('%Y%m%dT%H%M%SZ', time.gmtime())
 304      out = OUT_ROOT / utc
 305      out.mkdir(parents=True)  # a new folder; an existing one raises
 306      slot_dir = out / 'slots'
 307      slot_dir.mkdir()
 308      prov = provenance()
 309      (out / 'provenance.txt').write_text(prov)
 310      run = {'utc': utc, 'code_sha256': sha(Path(__file__).read_bytes()),
 311             'imports_sha256': {m.__name__: sha(Path(m.__file__).read_bytes()) for m in (b0, b0b, b2, b2.b1, mem1, vis3d)},
 312             'git_head': run_out('git', '-C', str(HERE), 'rev-parse', 'HEAD').strip(), 'pins': PINS,
 313             'set_sha256': {'b0': sha(s_bytes), 'test': b2.TEST_SHA256}, 'command': command(slot_dir),
 314             'provenance': prov, 'timings': 'NOT MEASUREMENTS', 'ram': {}}
 315      (out / 'run.json').write_text(json.dumps(run, indent=1) + '\n')
 316  
 317      def post(path, body):
 318          if body is None:  # GET
 319              with urllib.request.urlopen(f'http://127.0.0.1:{cr.PORT}{path}', timeout=30) as r:
 320                  return json.loads(r.read())
 321          return cr.post(path, body, timeout=b0.REQUEST_TIMEOUT_S)
 322  
 323      ph = {}
 324      with open(out / 'requests.jsonl', 'x') as rq, open(out / 'server.log', 'x') as log:
 325          def emit(row):
 326              rq.write(json.dumps(row) + '\n')
 327              rq.flush()
 328          killed, proc, exc = False, None, None
 329          try:
 330              proc = subprocess.Popen(run['command'], stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL, stdout=log, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,
 331                                      start_new_session=True)
 332              t0 = time.monotonic()
 333              while not cr.healthy():
 334                  if proc.poll() is not None or time.monotonic() - t0 > b0.LOAD_TIMEOUT_S:
 335                      raise RuntimeError(f'llama-server not healthy (exit {proc.poll()})')
 336                  time.sleep(0.1)
 337              run['server_pid'], run['server_comm'] = proc.pid, Path(f'/proc/{proc.pid}/comm').read_text().strip()
 338              run['ram']['after_load'] = ram(proc.pid)
 339              ph['K1'] = k1(post, s, proc.pid, emit)
 340              ph['K2'] = k2(post, s, proc.pid, emit)
 341              ph['K3'] = k3(post, refs['test'], proc.pid, emit)
 342              ph['K4'] = k4(post, proc.pid, emit)
 343              run['ram']['end'] = ram(proc.pid)
 344          except BaseException as e:  # recorded; the report and manifest are still written, then a stop re-raises
 345              exc = e
 346              run['exception'] = f'{type(e).__name__}: {e}'
 347          finally:
 348              if proc is not None and proc.poll() is None:
 349                  os.killpg(proc.pid, signal.SIGTERM)
 350                  try:
 351                      proc.wait(timeout=b0.TERM_WAIT_S)
 352                  except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
 353                      os.killpg(proc.pid, signal.SIGKILL)
 354                      proc.wait()
 355                      killed = True
 356              run.update(exit_code=proc.returncode if proc else None, killed=killed)
 357      if (slot_dir / b0b.SLOT_FILE).is_file():
 358          run['slot_file_bytes'] = (slot_dir / b0b.SLOT_FILE).stat().st_size
 359      print(finish(out, run, ph, refs) + f'\n{out}')
 360      if exc is not None and not isinstance(exc, Exception):
 361          raise exc
 362  
 363  
 364  if __name__ == '__main__':
 365      main()
END

  80  def image_payload(frame, jpeg=None):
  81      """VIS-0 vision request: image then text in one user message; greedy, at most 96 tokens, nothing cached."""
  82      url = 'data:image/jpeg;base64,' + (jpeg or base64.b64encode((FRAMES_DIR / f'{frame}.jpg').read_bytes()).decode())
  83      return {'messages': [{'role': 'user', 'content': [{'type': 'image_url', 'image_url': {'url': url}},
  84                                                        {'type': 'text', 'text': PROMPT}]}],
  85              'temperature': 0, 'top_k': 1, 'seed': 0, 'max_tokens': 96, 'cache_prompt': False, 'stream': False}
  86

  88  _post = cr.post
  89  
  90  
  91  def chat_post(path, body, timeout):
  92      """D: run_block posts to /completion; send the body to the chat endpoint instead (the server applies the GGUF
  93      Gemma 4 template) and return the reply and finish reason where run_block reads them."""
  94      assert path == '/completion', path
  95      data = _post('/v1/chat/completions', body, timeout)
  96      choice = data['choices'][0]
  97      return {**data, 'content': choice['message'].get('content') or '', 'stop_type': choice.get('finish_reason')}

 113              if not (FRAMES_DIR / f'{f}.jpg').is_file() or cr.sha256(FRAMES_DIR / f'{f}.jpg') != r['frame_sha256']]

 116  def check_hashes():
 117      got = {p: cr.sha256(p) if Path(p).is_file() else 'missing' for p in SHA256}
 118      build = {n: cr.sha256(Path(BIN) / n) if (Path(BIN) / n).is_file() else 'missing' for n in BUILD_SHA256}
 119      bad = [f'{p}: {got[p]} != {w}' for p, w in SHA256.items() if got[p] != w]
 120      bad += [f'{n}: {build[n]} != {w}' for n, w in BUILD_SHA256.items() if build[n] != w]
 121      if bad:
 122          raise SystemExit('SHA-256 mismatch: ' + '; '.join(bad))
 123      return got, build

BEGIN benchmark/mind_picker/b1_quality/b1.py (379 lines, excerpt lines 55-72)

  56  REQUEST_TIMEOUT_S, LOAD_TIMEOUT_S, TERM_WAIT_S = 300, 120, 30

  58  
  59  def inside_repo(path):
  60      p = Path(path).absolute()
  61      return any(q.exists() and os.path.samefile(q, REPO) for q in [p, *p.parents])  # bind mounts: same inode
  62  
  63  
  64  def load_set(path, want=SET_SHA256):
  65      """(set, bytes SHA-256): refused when the path is inside the repo (checked before reading) or the hash differs."""
  66      if inside_repo(path):
  67          raise SystemExit(f'refused: {path} is inside the repository (test-set custody)')
  68      data = Path(path).read_bytes()
  69      if want is not None and sha(data) != want:
  70          raise SystemExit(f'refused: {path} SHA-256 {sha(data)[:12]} != pinned {want[:12]}')
  71      return json.loads(data), sha(data)
  72

BEGIN detect_person.py (283 lines, excerpt lines 1-60)
   1  import sys, os, time, queue, threading
   2  import numpy as np
   3  import onnxruntime as ort
   4  from PIL import Image, ImageOps
   5  
   6  CLASSES = ['person','bicycle','car','motorbike','aeroplane','bus','train','truck',
   7  'boat','traffic light','fire hydrant','stop sign','parking meter','bench','bird',
   8  'cat','dog','horse','sheep','cow','elephant','bear','zebra','giraffe','backpack',
   9  'umbrella','handbag','tie','suitcase','frisbee','skis','snowboard','sports ball',
  10  'kite','baseball bat','baseball glove','skateboard','surfboard','tennis racket',
  11  'bottle','wine glass','cup','fork','knife','spoon','bowl','banana','apple',
  12  'sandwich','orange','broccoli','carrot','hot dog','pizza','donut','cake','chair',
  13  'couch','potted plant','bed','dining table','toilet','tv','laptop','mouse','remote',
  14  'keyboard','cell phone','microwave','oven','toaster','sink','refrigerator','book',
  15  'clock','vase','scissors','teddy bear','hair dryer','toothbrush']
  16  
  17  MODELS = {320: os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), 'yolo11s_320.onnx'),
  18            640: os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), 'yolo11s_640.onnx')}
  19  CONF  = 0.35
  20  IOU   = 0.45
  21  
  22  class Detector:
  23      def __init__(self):
  24          self.sessions = {size: ort.InferenceSession(path) for size, path in MODELS.items()}
  25  
  26      def detect(self, frame, size=320):
  27          if size not in self.sessions:
  28              raise ValueError('size must be 320 or 640')
  29          if not isinstance(frame, Image.Image):
  30              with Image.open(frame) as source:
  31                  frame = ImageOps.exif_transpose(source).convert('RGB')
  32          width, height = frame.size
  33          img = frame.resize((size, size)).convert('RGB')
  34          arr = np.array(img).astype(np.float32) / 255.0
  35          arr = arr.transpose(2, 0, 1)[np.newaxis]
  36          session = self.sessions[size]
  37          out = session.run(None, {session.get_inputs()[0].name: arr})[0][0].T
  38          by_class = {}
  39          for pred in out:
  40              scores = pred[4:]
  41              cls = int(np.argmax(scores))
  42              conf = float(scores[cls])
  43              if conf > CONF:
  44                  cx, cy, w, h = map(float, pred[:4])
  45                  box = (cx-w/2, cy-h/2, cx+w/2, cy+h/2)
  46                  by_class.setdefault(cls, []).append((box, conf))
  47          results = []
  48          for cls, items in by_class.items():
  49              for i in nms([v[0] for v in items], [v[1] for v in items], IOU):
  50                  box, conf = items[i]
  51                  x1, y1, x2, y2 = box
  52                  results.append({'class_name': CLASSES[cls], 'class_id': cls,
  53                                  'conf': conf,
  54                                  'box_xyxy': (max(0, min(width, x1*width/size)),
  55                                               max(0, min(height, y1*height/size)),
  56                                               max(0, min(width, x2*width/size)),
  57                                               max(0, min(height, y2*height/size))),
  58                                  'size_used': size})
  59          return sorted(results, key=lambda d: -d['conf'])
  60  
END

 196          i = order.pop(0)

 201  def scene_from_detections(detections, frame_width, frame_height):
 202      """Legacy tuple view used by navigation, stereo and text callers."""
 203      results = []
 204      seen = set()
 205      for d in detections:
 206          label = d['class_name']
 207          if label in seen:
 208              continue
 209          seen.add(label)
 210          x1, y1, x2, y2 = d['box_xyxy']
 211          cx, cy, w, h = (x1+x2)/2, (y1+y2)/2, x2-x1, y2-y1
 212          area = w*h/(frame_width*frame_height)
 213          pos = 'left' if cx < frame_width/3 else 'right' if cx > 2*frame_width/3 else 'center'
 214          dist = 'very close' if area>0.3 else 'close' if area>0.1 else 'medium' if area>0.03 else 'far'
 215          results.append((label, round(d['conf'], 2), pos, dist,
 216                          round(cx*640/frame_width), round(cy*640/frame_height),
 217                          round(w*640/frame_width), round(h*640/frame_height)))
 218      return results
 219  
 220  def detect_scene(image_path, size=640):

 121  def enrich_detections(raw_detections, frame_size):
 122      """Attach position and coarse distance bucket from detect_person.scene_from_detections to each detection."""
 123      width, height = frame_size
 124      enriched = []
 125      for d in raw_detections:
 126          _, _, pos, dist, *_ = detect_person.scene_from_detections([d], width, height)[0]
 127          enriched.append({
 128              'class_name': str(d['class_name']),
 129              'conf': float(d['conf']),
 130              'box_xyxy': [float(x) for x in d['box_xyxy']],
 131              'position': pos,
 132              'distance': dist,
 133          })
 134      return enriched

 497                  'frame_bytes': frame_path.stat().st_size,
 498                  'frame_size': list(decoded.size),
 499                  'detections': detections,
 500              })
 501          except Exception as e:
 502              failed_photos.append({'photo': rel, 'stage': 'frame_or_yolo', 'error': f'{type(e).__name__}: {e}'})
 503              log(f'ERROR on {rel}: {type(e).__name__}: {e}')
 504  
 505      qualifying_names = [item['photo'] for item in staged if len(item['detections']) >= 2]
 506      blind_key = assign_blind_key(qualifying_names, seed=BLIND_SEED)
 507  
 508      photo_records = []
 509      for item in staged:
 510          rel = item['photo']
 511          detections = item['detections']
 512          decoded = frames_by_photo[rel]
 513          if len(detections) < 2:
 514              m2_res = run_m2_once(head, decoded, detections)
 515              photo_records.append({
 516                  'photo': rel,
 517                  'source_sha256': item['source_sha256'],
 518                  'source_bytes': item['source_bytes'],
 519                  'frame_file': item['frame_file'],
 520                  'frame_sha256': item['frame_sha256'],
 521                  'frame_bytes': item['frame_bytes'],
 522                  'frame_size': item['frame_size'],
 523                  'detection_count': len(detections),
 524                  'classes': [d['class_name'] for d in detections],
 525                  'm2_ran': False,
 526                  'm2_shown_text': m2_res['shown_text'],
 527                  'timing_label': TIMING_LABEL,
 528              })
 529              log(f'{rel}: <2 boxes ({len(detections)}), skipped M2 and Gemma')
 530              continue
END
